<script>
  // Force the webfont fetch — some sandboxed previews don't trigger it from
  // the <link> alone, which leaves the display face on a generic-serif fallback.
  (function () {
    function load() {
      if (!document.fonts || !document.fonts.load) return;
      ["400 1em \"Cooper Black\"", "700 1em \"Spectral\"", "600 1em \"Spectral\"", "500 1em \"Spectral\""]
        .forEach(function (f) { try { document.fonts.load(f); } catch (e) {} });
    }
    load();
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", load);
  })();
</script>
<style>
  /* the real Cooper Black, self-hosted (user-provided, licensed) */

  // the <link> alone, which leaves the display face on a generic-serif fallback.
  (function () {
    function load() {
      if (!document.fonts || !document.fonts.load) return;
      ["400 1em \"Cooper Black\"", "700 1em \"Spectral\"", "600 1em \"Spectral\"", "500 1em \"Spectral\""]
        .forEach(function (f) { try { document.fonts.load(f); } catch (e) {} });
    }
    load();
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", load);
  })();
</script>

  /* ================================================================
     PETCO PARK — "Dark Urban Monograph"
     Blurb Large Landscape 13 x 11 in spread. 2550 x 1088 px @ 100ppi.
     Export 3x = 7650 x 3264. Center split / fold at x = 1275.
     A premium redevelopment-district monograph: dark charcoal paper across both
     pages, the right page read as an architectural drawing pinned to a graphite
     board. Steel drafting rules — not masonry — divide four warehouse-derived
     bays. Brick appears only as a few small accents where the Western Metal
     Supply story is referenced. Material mix ≈ 60% charcoal / 25% steel-graphite
     / 10% sandstone / 5% brick.
       LEFT  page = near full-bleed dusk district aerial + a slim black-steel
         title rail (vertically mounted industrial signage) on the outer edge.
       RIGHT page = four bays as drawing fields:
         A Identity & Specifications (+ field protractor instrument)
         B Stadium Context (narrative sheet)
         C Architectural Story (three steel window openings)

  * { box-sizing: border-box; }

    font-family: "Spectral", Georgia, serif;
    color: #23211C;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  }

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------------
     THREE RIVERS STADIUM — Blurb Large Landscape, two-page spread.
     2550 x 1088 px (25.50 x 10.88 in @ 100ppi). Export 3x = 7650 x 3264 px.
     Center fold x=1275. Bleed-only outer 12.5px; safe content >= 37.5px inside trim.
     Gutter no-content zone x=1237.5-1312.5.
     Left page = full-bleed hero. Right page = Stadium Section + Visit Section.
     ---------------------------------------------------------------- */
  .trs-spread {
    position: relative;
    width: 2550px; height: 1088px;
    overflow: hidden;
    --ink: #23211C;
    --ink-2: #56524A;
    --ink-3: #847F73;
    --paper: #E7E4DC;
    --paper-hi: #EFEBE2;
    --paper-lo: #DAD5C8;
    --rule: #CBC6B8;
    --rule-strong: #A8A290;
    --black: #161513;
    --black-2: #0F0E0C;
    --gold: #C0922E;
